##########################################
######   POD COMBAT MOVE EFFECTS   ######
##########################################
# Individual scripted effects for all 434 active (non-passive) combat moves.
# Each effect spends AP, clears result flags, sets log metadata, applies
# damage / healing / buffs / debuffs, then pushes the combat log entry.
#
# Damage functions (defined in pod_combat_effects.txt):
#   pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect          — hit roll -> guard -> soak -> HP
#   pod_combat_deal_supernatural_hp_damage_effect      — auto-hit, no guard, soak -> HP
#   pod_combat_deal_physical_bypass_guard_hp_damage_effect — hit roll, skip guard, soak -> HP
#   pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect        — like physical, soak halved
#   pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect    — like supernatural, soak halved
#   pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_bypass_guard_hp_damage_effect — physical agg + bypass guard
#   pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect               — direct morale damage
#   pod_combat_restore_hp_effect                       — heal HP
#   pod_combat_restore_morale_effect                   — heal morale
#   pod_combat_spend_ap_effect                         — spend AP


###### BASE PHYSICAL (4) ######

# ── 1. Strike ───────────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_strike_effect = {
	# Strike — Physical/Superficial — 3 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 3 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 1 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:strike }

	pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 2. Heavy Blow ───────────────────────────────────────────

# ── 4. Reckless Charge ──────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_reckless_charge_effect = {
	# Reckless Charge — Physical/Superficial — 5 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 5 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 4 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:reckless_charge }

	pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }

	# Self-damage (always hits, no guard, no soak)
	# Victory is checked at save_workspace time; no in-pipeline call needed.
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = new_hp
		value = {
			value = var:pod_combat_player_hp
			subtract = 1
			min = 0
		}
	}
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hp value = scope:new_hp }
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}


###### BASE TACTICAL (4) ######

# ── 5. Guard ────────────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_guard_effect = {
	# Guard — None/None — 2 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 2 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 5 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:guard }

	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_guarding value = yes }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 6 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:feint }

	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_opponent_feinted value = yes }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_guarding
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 10. Torch Sweep ─────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_torch_sweep_effect = {
	# Torch Sweep — Fire/Superficial — 5 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 5 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 159 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:torch_sweep }
	# Bypass dodge: attack is undodgeable
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_dodging

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_fire_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_fire_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_fire_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_fire_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_fire_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 11. Gold Arrow Shot ─────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_gold_arrow_shot_effect = {
	# Gold Arrow Shot — Gold/Superficial — 4 AP, 15 Gold
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 4 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		remove_short_term_gold = 15
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 160 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:gold_arrow_shot }

	pod_combat_deal_gold_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

###### QIAO LONG LING (2) ######

# ── 24. Eyes of the Dragon ──────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_eyes_of_the_dragon_effect = {
	# Eyes of the Dragon — Mental/None — 2 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 2 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 140 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:eyes_of_the_dragon }
	# Bypass stealth: strip stealth state and reverse hit bonus
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_stealthed
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = reduced_hit
		value = { value = var:pod_combat_opponent_hit_chance subtract = 30 min = 20 }
	}
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_opponent_hit_chance value = scope:reduced_hit }

	pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
	# Stealth: negate next attack + hit chance bonus for next strike
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_stealthed value = yes }
	change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_chance add = 30 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 25. Mantle of Heaven ────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_mantle_of_heaven_effect = {
	# Mantle of Heaven — Mental/None — 4 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 4 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 141 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:mantle_of_heaven }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 36. Sisters' Hosanna ────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_sisters_hosanna_effect = {
	# Sisters' Hosanna — None/None — 2 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 2 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 152 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:sisters_hosanna }

	# AoE ally: apply to every alive same-team slot.
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c0_char var:pod_combat_c0_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 0 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 0 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c0_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c0_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c0_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c1_char var:pod_combat_c1_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 1 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 1 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c1_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c1_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c1_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c2_char var:pod_combat_c2_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 2 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 2 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c2_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c2_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c2_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c3_char var:pod_combat_c3_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 3 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 3 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c3_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c3_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c3_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c4_char var:pod_combat_c4_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 4 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 4 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c4_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c4_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c4_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	}

# ── 53. Judgment of Heaven ──────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_judgement_of_heaven_effect = {
	# Judgment of Heaven — Holy/Aggravated — 6 AP, 20 Stress
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 6 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		add_stress = 20
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 173 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:judgement_of_heaven }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:hellfire_inferno }
	# Bypass dodge: attack is undodgeable
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_dodging

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 6 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}

	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal

	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:storm_apocalypse }
	# Bypass dodge: attack is undodgeable
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_dodging

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }

	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}


###### SORCERY OF HEALING (3) ######

# ── 71. Blessed Healing ─────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_blessed_healing_effect = {
	# Blessed Healing — None/Superficial — 3 AP, 20 Stress
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 3 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		add_stress = 20
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 311 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:blessed_healing }

	pod_combat_restore_hp_superficial_effect = { SIDE = player AMOUNT = 3 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal

	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:nightmare_incarnate }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {

	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:death_incarnate }

	pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }

	# Drain heal (only if the attack landed — not soaked)
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked }
		}
		pod_combat_restore_hp_aggravated_effect = { SIDE = player AMOUNT = 2 }
	}
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 85. Spirit Fire Inferno ─────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_spirit_fire_inferno_effect = {
	# Spirit Fire Inferno — Fire/Superficial — 6 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 6 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 175 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:spirit_fire_inferno }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_fire_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_fire_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_fire_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_fire_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_fire_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 9 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:bite }

	pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }

	# Drain heal (only if the attack landed)
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed }
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked }
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked }
		}
		pod_combat_restore_hp_superficial_effect = { SIDE = player AMOUNT = 1 }
	}
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 89. Blood Surge ─────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_blood_surge_effect = {
	# Blood Surge — None/None — 2 AP, 1 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 2 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_discipline_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 10 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:blood_surge }


	# Hit buff medium (combat) via tier system
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_combat_tier value = 2 }
	pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

}

# ── 100. Brutal Feed ────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_brutal_feed_effect = {
	# Brutal Feed — Physical/Superficial — 4 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 4 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 13 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:brutal_feed }

	pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }

	# Drain heal (only if the attack landed)
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed }
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked }
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked }
		}
		pod_combat_restore_hp_superficial_effect = { SIDE = player AMOUNT = 2 }
	}
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 101. Earthshock ─────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_earthshock_effect = {
	# Earthshock — Physical/Superficial — 5 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 5 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 14 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:earthshock }
	# Bypass dodge: attack is undodgeable
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_dodging

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 103. Dread Gaze ─────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_dread_gaze_effect = {
	# Dread Gaze — Mental/None — 3 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 3 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 24 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:dread_gaze }
	# Bypass stealth: strip stealth state and reverse hit bonus
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_stealthed
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = reduced_hit
		value = { value = var:pod_combat_opponent_hit_chance subtract = 30 min = 20 }
	}
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_opponent_hit_chance value = scope:reduced_hit }

	pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
	# Stealth: negate next attack + hit chance bonus for next strike
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_stealthed value = yes }
	change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_chance add = 30 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 34 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:vanish }

	# Stealth: negate next attack + hit chance bonus for next strike
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_stealthed value = yes }
	change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_chance add = 30 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 116. Drawing Out the Beast ──────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_drawing_out_the_beast_effect = {
	# Drawing Out the Beast — Physical/Aggravated — 4 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 4 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 37 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:drawing_out_the_beast }

	pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }

	# Self-damage (always hits, no guard, no soak)
	# Victory is checked at save_workspace time; no in-pipeline call needed.
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = new_hp
		value = {
			value = var:pod_combat_player_hp
			subtract = 1
			min = 0
		}
	}
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hp value = scope:new_hp }
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 122. Ahriman's Demesne ──────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_ahrimans_demesne_effect = {
	# Ahriman's Demesne — Mental/None — 4 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 4 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 46 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:ahrimans_demesne }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 128. Cobra Fangs ────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_cobra_fangs_effect = {
	# Cobra Fangs — Physical/Aggravated — 4 AP, 1 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 4 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_discipline_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 49 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:cobra_fangs }

	pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 129. Breath of the Basilisk ─────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_breath_of_basilisk_effect = {
	# Breath of the Basilisk — Supernatural/Aggravated — 5 AP, 2 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 5 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_discipline_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 50 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:breath_of_basilisk }
	# Bypass dodge: attack is undodgeable
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_dodging

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 358 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:blood_rage }


	# Hit buff medium (combat) via tier system
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_combat_tier value = 2 }
	pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 145. Theft of Vitae ─────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_theft_of_vitae_effect = {
	# Theft of Vitae — Supernatural/Superficial — 4 AP, 1 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 4 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_discipline_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 359 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:theft_of_vitae }

	pod_combat_deal_supernatural_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }

	# Drain heal (only if the attack landed — not soaked)
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked }
		}
		pod_combat_restore_hp_superficial_effect = { SIDE = player AMOUNT = 2 }
	}
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 151. Comrades at Arms ───────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_comrades_at_arms_effect = {
	# Comrades at Arms — None/None — 4 AP, 2 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 4 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_discipline_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 357 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:comrades_at_arms }

	# AoE ally: apply to every alive same-team slot.
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c0_char var:pod_combat_c0_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 0 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 0 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c0_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c0_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c0_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c1_char var:pod_combat_c1_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 1 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 1 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c1_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c1_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c1_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c2_char var:pod_combat_c2_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 2 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 2 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c2_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c2_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c2_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c3_char var:pod_combat_c3_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 3 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 3 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c3_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c3_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c3_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c4_char var:pod_combat_c4_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 4 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 4 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c4_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c4_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c4_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 159. Massive Flames ─────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_massive_flames_effect = {
	# Massive Flames — Fire/Aggravated — 6 AP, 2 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 6 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_discipline_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 365 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:massive_flames }
	# Bypass dodge: attack is undodgeable
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_dodging

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 174. Dark Invocation ────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_dark_invocation_effect = {
	# Dark Invocation — Mental/None — 4 AP, 2 Blood, 20 Stress
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 4 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_discipline_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 }
		add_stress = 20
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 384 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:dark_invocation }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}

		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }

###### EVIL EYE (2) ######

# ── 178. Humiliation ────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_humiliation_effect = {
	# Humiliation — Mental/None — 3 AP, 1 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 3 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_discipline_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 388 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:humiliation }
	# Bypass stealth: strip stealth state and reverse hit bonus
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_stealthed
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = reduced_hit
		value = { value = var:pod_combat_opponent_hit_chance subtract = 30 min = 20 }
	}
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_opponent_hit_chance value = scope:reduced_hit }

	pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }

	# Hit debuff medium (round) via tier system
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_opponent_hit_debuff_round_tier value = 2 }
	pod_combat_recalc_hit_opponent_effect = yes
	# Stealth: negate next attack + hit chance bonus for next strike
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_stealthed value = yes }
	change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_chance add = 30 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 389 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:eye_that_wounds }
	# Bypass stealth: strip stealth state and reverse hit bonus
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_stealthed
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = reduced_hit
		value = { value = var:pod_combat_opponent_hit_chance subtract = 30 min = 20 }
	}
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_opponent_hit_chance value = scope:reduced_hit }

	pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
	# Stealth: negate next attack + hit chance bonus for next strike
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_stealthed value = yes }
	change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_chance add = 30 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	}

		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }

	}

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 390 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:endurance_of_stone }


	# Soak buff large (combat) via tier system

	pod_combat_recalc_soak_player_effect = yes
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }

}

# ── 185. Doom Tide ──────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_doom_tide_effect = {
	# Doom Tide — Supernatural/Aggravated — 6 AP, 2 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 6 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_discipline_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 395 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:doom_tide }
	# Bypass dodge: attack is undodgeable
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_dodging

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 400 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:grief_incarnate }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
