	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble

# ── 206. Shambling Hordes ───────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_shambling_hordes_effect = {
	# Shambling Hordes — Physical/Aggravated — 6 AP, 2 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 6 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_discipline_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 327 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:shambling_hordes }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes

# ── 208. Reveal the Catene ──────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_reveal_the_catene_effect = {
	# Reveal the Catene — Supernatural/Superficial — 4 AP, 1 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 4 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_discipline_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 329 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:reveal_the_catene }
	# Bypass stealth: strip stealth state and reverse hit bonus
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_stealthed
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = reduced_hit
		value = { value = var:pod_combat_opponent_hit_chance subtract = 30 min = 20 }
	}
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_opponent_hit_chance value = scope:reduced_hit }

	pod_combat_deal_supernatural_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
	# Stealth: negate next attack + hit chance bonus for next strike
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_stealthed value = yes }
	change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_chance add = 30 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 218. Soul Drain ─────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_soul_drain_effect = {
	# Soul Drain — Supernatural/Aggravated — 3 AP, 1 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 3 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_discipline_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 339 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:soul_drain }

	pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }

	# Drain heal (only if the attack landed — not soaked)
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked }
		}
		pod_combat_restore_hp_aggravated_effect = { SIDE = player AMOUNT = 1 }
	}
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 221. Lament of Dhainu ───────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_lament_of_dhainu_effect = {
	# Lament of Dhainu — Mental/None — 5 AP, 2 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 5 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_discipline_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 342 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:lament_of_dhainu }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 258. War Form ───────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_war_form_effect = {
	# War Form — None/None — 3 AP, 1 Gnosis
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 3 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_gift_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 78 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:war_form }


	# Hit buff medium (combat) via tier system
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_combat_tier value = 2 }
	pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

pod_combat_use_battle_howl_effect = {
	# Battle Howl — Mental/None — 2 AP, 1 Gnosis
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 2 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_gift_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 80 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:battle_howl }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}


###### WEREWOLF GENERAL (2) ######

# ── 261. Heart Claw ─────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_heart_claw_attack_effect = {
	# Heart Claw — Physical/Aggravated — 5 AP, 1 Gnosis
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 5 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_gift_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 99 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:heart_claw_attack }

	pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }

	# Drain heal (only if the attack landed)
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed }
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked }
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked }
		}
		pod_combat_restore_hp_aggravated_effect = { SIDE = player AMOUNT = 1 }
	}
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 270. Howl of the Banshee ────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_howl_of_the_banshee_effect = {
	# Howl of the Banshee — Mental/None — 3 AP, 1 Gnosis
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 3 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_gift_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 86 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:howl_of_the_banshee }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }

		POD_gift_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 199 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:hootenanny }

	# AoE ally: apply to every alive same-team slot.
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c0_char var:pod_combat_c0_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 0 }

		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 0 }

# ── 295. Fenris Bite ────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_fenris_bite_effect = {
	# Fenris Bite — Physical/Aggravated — 6 AP, 2 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 6 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_gift_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 207 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:fenris_bite }

	pod_combat_deal_physical_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }

	# Drain heal (only if the attack landed)
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed }
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked }
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked }
		}
		pod_combat_restore_hp_aggravated_effect = { SIDE = player AMOUNT = 1 }
	}
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 299. Howl of Death ──────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_howl_of_death_effect = {
	# Howl of Death — Mental/None — 4 AP, 1 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 4 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_gift_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 211 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:howl_of_death }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).

		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }

###### TRIBE SIMBA (3) ######

# ── 316. Bastet Majesty ─────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_bastet_majesty_effect = {
	# Bastet Majesty — Mental/None — 3 AP, 1 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 3 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_gift_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 235 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:bastet_majesty }

	pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 320. Madness of Crowds ──────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_madness_of_crowds_effect = {
	# Madness of Crowds — Mental/None — 3 AP, 1 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 3 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_gift_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 239 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:madness_of_crowds }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }

	}

		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }

###### SOLAR DECORATED SUN (1) ######

# ── 333. Pack Tactics ───────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_pack_tactics_effect = {
	# Pack Tactics — None/None — 3 AP, 1 Blood
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 3 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 186 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:pack_tactics }

	# AoE ally: apply to every alive same-team slot.
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c0_char var:pod_combat_c0_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 0 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 0 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c0_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c0_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c0_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c1_char var:pod_combat_c1_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 1 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 1 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c1_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c1_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c1_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c2_char var:pod_combat_c2_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 2 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 2 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c2_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c2_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c2_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c3_char var:pod_combat_c3_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 3 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 3 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c3_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c3_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c3_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c4_char var:pod_combat_c4_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 4 }
		
		# Hit buff medium (round) via tier system
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_buff_round_tier value = 2 }
		pod_combat_recalc_hit_player_effect = yes
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 4 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c4_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c4_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c4_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 193 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:shed_scales }

	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }

# ── 339. Thousand Arrows ────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_thousand_arrows_effect = {
	# Thousand Arrows — Physical/Superficial — 6 AP, 2 Gnosis
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 6 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		POD_gift_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }
		change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_energy_used add = 1 }
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 194 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:thousand_arrows }

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c6_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c6_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c6_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c7_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c7_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c7_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 8 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c8_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c8_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c8_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		pod_combat_deal_physical_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 9 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c9_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c9_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c9_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	# Reload original target after AoE to prevent workspace scramble
	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		add_stress = 40
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 249 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:wave_of_mutilation }
	# Bypass dodge: attack is undodgeable
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_dodging

	# AoE: apply to every alive opposing-team slot (actor-aware).
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }
		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
		pod_combat_save_target_to_slot_effect = { SLOT = 5 }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:pod_combat_c5_hp <= 0 var:pod_combat_c5_morale <= 0 } } set_variable = { name = pod_combat_c5_alive value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1 }
		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 6 }

		pod_combat_load_target_from_slot_effect = { SLOT = 7 }

		set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }

	pod_combat_load_workspace_effect = yes

# ── 362. Coherent Light ─────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_coherent_light_effect = {
	# Coherent Light — Sunlight/Superficial — 5 AP, 40 Stress
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 5 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_current_actor var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		add_stress = 40
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 259 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:coherent_light }
	# Bypass stealth: strip stealth state and reverse hit bonus
	remove_variable = pod_combat_opponent_is_stealthed
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = reduced_hit
		value = { value = var:pod_combat_opponent_hit_chance subtract = 30 min = 20 }
	}
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_opponent_hit_chance value = scope:reduced_hit }

	pod_combat_deal_sunlight_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 4 }
	# Stealth: negate next attack + hit chance bonus for next strike
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_stealthed value = yes }
	change_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hit_chance add = 30 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

		add_stress = 40
	}
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 266 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:extinguish_life }

	pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 5 }

	# Drain heal (only if the attack landed — not soaked)
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked }
		}
		pod_combat_restore_hp_aggravated_effect = { SIDE = player AMOUNT = 2 }
	}
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

		limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_energy_used }

	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged

	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 411 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }

# ── 424. Discorporate ───────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_discorporate_effect = {
	# Discorporate — None/None — 2 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 2 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 460 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:discorporate }

	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_is_dodging value = yes }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 425. Spirit Touch ───────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_spirit_touch_effect = {
	# Spirit Touch — Mental/None — 2 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 2 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 461 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:spirit_touch }

	pod_combat_deal_morale_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }

	# Soak debuff small (round) via tier system
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_opponent_soak_debuff_round_tier value = 1 }
	pod_combat_recalc_soak_opponent_effect = yes
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

###### WYRM SPIRIT (2) ######

# ── 428. Bane Maw ───────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_bane_maw_effect = {
	# Bane Maw — Supernatural/Aggravated — 3 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 3 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 464 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:bane_maw }

	pod_combat_deal_supernatural_aggravated_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 3 }

	# Self-damage (always hits, no guard, no soak)
	# Victory is checked at save_workspace time; no in-pipeline call needed.
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = new_hp
		value = {
			value = var:pod_combat_player_hp
			subtract = 1
			min = 0
		}
	}
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_player_hp value = scope:new_hp }
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 433. Devour Hope ────────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_devour_hope_effect = {
	# Devour Hope — Supernatural/Superficial — 3 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 3 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 469 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:devour_hope }

	pod_combat_deal_supernatural_hp_damage_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent AMOUNT = 2 }

	# Drain heal (only if the attack landed — not soaked)
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked }
		}
		pod_combat_restore_hp_superficial_effect = { SIDE = player AMOUNT = 2 }
	}
	pod_combat_check_retaliation_effect = { TARGET_SIDE = opponent ACTOR_SIDE = player }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}

# ── 434. Oblivion's Grip ────────────────────────────────────
pod_combat_use_oblivions_grip_effect = {
	# Oblivion's Grip — None/None — 3 AP
	pod_combat_spend_ap_effect = { AMOUNT = 3 }
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_blocked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_missed
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_dodged
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliated
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_retaliate_soaked
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_damage
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_heal
	remove_variable = pod_combat_last_was_morale_heal
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_log_msg_id value = 470 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_actor_side value = var:pod_combat_current_turn_side }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_action value = flag:oblivions_grip }


	# Debuff: drain 2 AP from target's next turn
	change_variable = { name = pod_combat_opponent_ap_drain add = 2 }
	set_variable = { name = pod_combat_last_damage value = 0 }

	pod_combat_push_log_effect = yes
}
